## Shard 0

# SEO and GEO audit: LexiClash

**Scope limit:** the material I was given is docs, nightly artifacts, seo-daily reports and skill files. It does not include `app/sitemap.ts`, `public/llms.txt`, `robots.ts`, `lib/seo/hreflang.ts` or any `page.tsx`. I only give exact diffs where the broken line is visible. Everywhere else I say "verify, then apply", and I have not invented line numbers.

## 1. IndexNow pings are wrong or never sent, and Bing is about half your clicks
- **Evidence:**
  - `docs/seo-daily/2026-05-05.md:87` uses `"host": "lexiclash.com"`, but the site is `lexiclash.live`.
  - `docs/seo-daily/2026-05-02.md:75-76` uses host `lexiclash.live` with a `urlList` of `www.lexiclash.live` URLs. IndexNow rejects URLs whose host does not match.
  - `docs/nightly/artifacts/lane-06-seo-2026-08-16.md:22-24` says IndexNow "needs CRON_SECRET, not available in this shell". `lane-06-seo-2026-08-09.md:3` notes that Bing shows zero presence for the top query.
  - Bing parity gaps grew from 8 queries (05-02) to 263 (08-16), and each run's next steps still list IndexNow as unsent.
- **Impact:** Bing was 51 of 98 clicks in May. The queries missing there include the 12k-impression Spanish scrabble cluster, so you are missing the Bing and Copilot index for your highest-volume pages.
- **Diff** (template in the report generator, then run it once from a normal shell with the public endpoint):
```diff
-    "host": "lexiclash.com",
+    "host": "www.lexiclash.live",
     "key": "<INDEXNOW_KEY>",
+    "keyLocation": "https://www.lexiclash.live/<INDEXNOW_KEY>.txt",
```
- **Also check:** the Bing property is registered as `https://lexiclash.live/` (`.claude/skills/seo-improve/SKILL.md:42`) while indexed URLs are `www.`. Pick one host, 301 the other, and register that host in Bing.

## 2. Spanish scrabble page: about 25 title rewrites, CTR still around 0.3%
- **Evidence:**
  - The page `/es/juego-de-palabras-multijugador` appears in nearly every artifact from 05-29 to 08-13.
  - `lane-06-seo-2026-06-18.md:11` shows the earlier "sin turnos" copy was anti-intent.
  - `lane-06-seo-2026-10-12.md:3` says three prior rewrites "failed to move it".
  - `lane-06-seo-2026-08-16.md:9-15` and `2026-09-26.md:5` give the same diagnosis: not a meta problem, probably a SERP or brand-intent issue.
- **Impact:** about 12k impressions per 28 days at 0.3% CTR. Every rewrite also resets Google's evaluation.
- **Fix:**
  - Stop editing title and description. Keep `seo-freeze.json` in force for this URL.
  - Query the intent split in GSC, filtering for brand versus generic.
  - Aim the new content at non-brand intent ("juego de palabras online gratis", from `2026-07-04.md:33-34`) on a separate URL, instead of fighting Hasbro's brand queries on this one.
- **Diff** (to the nightly prompt, `scripts/nightly/prompts/06-seo.md`, not shown): add the rule `Do not change <title>/<meta> on a URL edited in the last 28d unless GSC position moved >3.`

## 3. Locale pages that are both noindexed and advertised through hreflang
- **Evidence:**
  - `docs/2026-06-08-education-for-schools-leads-seo-geo-spec.md:26` says non-EN locales get `robots:{index:false}` plus "hreflang back to EN".
  - `docs/seo-daily/2026-05-13.md:100` says the noindexed pages also canonical to `/en/`.
  - The same report (`:100-110`) shows noindexed SV pages still being linked internally and still surfacing at pos 19.9 in the US SERP.
- **Impact:** noindex plus canonical-to-another-URL is a conflicting signal, and hreflang to a noindexed alternate is ignored. This wastes crawl budget and risks a repeat of the 2026-05-20 incident (~900 noindexed URLs, from `docs/2026-08-30-education-landing-seo-handoff.md:41`).
- **Fix:** choose one mechanism per page, as below. Verify in `lib/seo/hreflang.ts` that `addForLocaleOnly` is respected by `sitemap.ts` and by `generateMetadata`.
```diff
- // noindexed non-EN: robots noindex + canonical→/en + hreflang→/en
+ // noindexed non-EN: robots noindex only, self-canonical, NO hreflang, NOT in sitemap
```

## 4. Education cluster went from 6 to 72 sitemap URLs with regional hreflang
- **Evidence:** `docs/2026-08-30-education-landing-seo-handoff.md:39-43` (en-GB, es-MX, ru-RU variants) and `:16`.
- **Impact:** this is the cluster most relevant to first revenue (for-schools leads). If coverage errors spike, indexing of the whole education cluster suffers.
- **Fix:** a two-week check in GSC → Pages filtered to `/education/`. Add an automated alert for the Pages report, and keep the hreflang self-reference and reciprocity test in CI. No code diff until the data says otherwise.

## 5. The nightly SEO pipeline decides from corrupted data
- **Evidence:**
  - `lane-06-seo-2026-08-13.md:7`: a shared `/tmp/seo-data.json` was overwritten by a lf-finance.co.il pull.
  - `lane-06-seo-2026-09-09.md:6`: GSC credentials drifted to lf-finance.co.il mid-run.
  - `lane-06-seo-2026-07-23.md:3`: the pull hit the wrong ADC property.
- **Impact:** every rewrite made on a corrupted night is a guess. This probably feeds the thrash in #2.
- **Diff** (`pull.py`, `analyze.py` and `report.py` from the `seo-daily` skill, not shown; the pattern is):
```diff
-OUT = "/tmp/seo-data.json"
+OUT = os.path.join(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="seo-"), "data.json")
+assert site_url == args.site, "GSC property drifted"
```

## 6. The report generator recommends rating schema, which conflicts with your own rule
- **Evidence:**
  - `docs/seo-daily/2026-05-05.md:47` and `2026-05-18.md:28` say "Add SoftwareApplication+rating".
  - `.claude/skills/seo-improve/SKILL.md:64` forbids fake `aggregateRating`.
- **Impact:** an unattended lane following the report could ship self-serving or fake review markup, which risks a structured-data manual action.
- **Diff** (report template):
```diff
-Add SoftwareApplication+rating, year in title
+Add SoftwareApplication (no aggregateRating unless real reviews), year in title
```

- **Evidence:** `lane-06-2026-06-18.md:5` through `lane-06-2026-07-19.md:9`, and `07-10.md:7`. The target is `המילה היומית` at pos 8.1–8.7 with 450–640 impressions.
- **Conflict to resolve:** `06-11` and `06-12` claim links were added (`HomepageContentSection.tsx`, `lexiclash-neged-wordle`), yet later runs still list the link as missing.

- **Evidence:** nothing in the provided files mentions an API, MCP server, CLI or OpenAPI spec. `llms.txt` is referenced (`SKILL.md:60`, handoff `:24`) but only as a text file of query-to-URL mappings (`2026-06-08 spec:52`).
- **Impact:** the "Kahoot alternative for classrooms" intent is increasingly resolved by agents. With no machine-readable way to create a classroom or join code, you lose citations that could carry a CTA.

  - Link it from `llms.txt`, together with a `/.well-known/` OpenAPI file.

```diff
+rows = [r for r in rows if len(r["query"]) <= 80]
```

## Shard 1

# SEO/GEO audit: lexiclash.live

**Scope limit:** the only files provided are `docs/seo-daily/*.md`. I did not see `page.tsx`, `sitemap.ts`, `robots.ts`, the translation files or any `public/` files. Every `path:line` below points at the evidence in those reports. Diffs for source files are written against file paths the reports name, and should be checked against the real code before applying.

## 1. The Spanish page has been rewritten about 12 times, so no change can be measured
`docs/seo-daily/2026-06-18.md:36` (also `2026-05-31.md:33`, `2026-06-10.md:41`, `2026-06-13.md:92`, `2026-06-14.md:110`, `2026-06-16.md:96`)

- **Evidence:** `/es/juego-de-palabras-multijugador` gets about 28k impressions and 178 clicks (`2026-06-16.md:69`). Its title and description were changed on 05-22, 05-26, 05-28, 05-31, 06-01, 06-06, 06-10, 06-12, 06-13, 06-14 and 06-18.
- **Contradiction in the copy:** "no por turnos" was added on 05-31 (`2026-05-31.md:33`) as the click trigger. It was removed on 06-18 as an anti-intent signal (`2026-06-18.md:36`).
- **Impact:** Google re-snippets after every change, and CTR stays at 0.3% at position 5.5. That is the largest click pool on the site (about 8k impressions on `scrabble online` alone). At 2% CTR it is worth roughly 130 clicks per 28 days.
- **Fix:** freeze the title and description for 28 days. Use the plain-intent variant:
```diff
- title: 'Scrabble Online Español 2026 — Gratis, Sin App | LexiClash'
+ title: 'Scrabble Online Gratis en Español — Juega con Amigos | LexiClash'
+ // FROZEN until 2026-07-20. Do not edit without a GSC before/after note.
```
- **Note:** the "2026" suffix works against you. The year-tagged queries fell 80–100% (`2026-05-28.md:126`).

## 2. Agents have no way to reach the product, and no llms.txt is mentioned anywhere
Evidence of absence: no report mentions `llms.txt`, an API, an MCP server or a CLI.

- **Impact:** AI answers for "best word games" cite lists rather than products. The `i am a 18-24 or 25-34 year old…` prompt-style queries already rank at pos 1.9–3.6 on `/en/leaderboard` and `/en/best-online-word-games` (`2026-06-11.md:77-80`). That means LLM-style queries are landing on you, and there is nothing machine-readable to hand them.
- **Fix:** add `fe-next/public/llms.txt` (and a short `llms-full.txt`):
```diff
+ # LexiClash
+ > Free real-time multiplayer word game (Boggle/Scrabble-style) in the browser. No download, no signup. 5 languages: en, he, sv, es, ja.
+ ## Play
+ - [Multiplayer](https://www.lexiclash.live/en/multiplayer-word-game-online): create a room, invite by link, 2–50 players
+ - [Daily puzzle](https://www.lexiclash.live/en/daily-word-wheel)
+ - [Leaderboard](https://www.lexiclash.live/en/leaderboard)
+ ## Guides
+ - [Best free browser word games](https://www.lexiclash.live/en/best-online-word-games)
+ - [Boggle vs Scrabble](https://www.lexiclash.live/en/blog/boggle-vs-scrabble)
+ ## Locale landing pages
+ - [ES](https://www.lexiclash.live/es/juego-de-palabras-multijugador) · [SV](https://www.lexiclash.live/sv/swedish-multiplayer-word-game) · [HE](https://www.lexiclash.live/he/daily)
```
- **Follow-up:** the one-link room invite is a good agent hook. A public `POST /api/rooms` that returns a join URL, documented in `llms.txt`, would let an assistant start a game for a user.

## 3. A live Bing Webmaster API key is committed in the docs
`docs/seo-daily/2026-05-21.md:54`

- **Impact:** anyone can submit or spam URLs against your Bing property. It also teaches future agents to copy the pattern.
- **Fix:** rotate the key in Bing WMT now, then:
```diff
- curl -X POST "https://ssl.bing.com/webmaster/api.svc/json/SubmitUrlbatch?apikey=c13df4f2164643cabd986b652269b1c5" \
+ curl -X POST "https://ssl.bing.com/webmaster/api.svc/json/SubmitUrlbatch?apikey=$BING_WMT_API_KEY" \
```
Then purge it from git history.

## 4. IndexNow is run by hand with inconsistent hosts, and the "Bing parity gap" is probably a measurement artifact
`2026-05-20.md:120`, `2026-05-22.md:88`, `2026-06-12.md:136`, `2026-06-17.md:61`

- **Inconsistent hosts:** the commands use `"host":"lexiclash.live"` in one place and `www.lexiclash.live` in another. Several omit `keyLocation`, and one uses the wrong endpoint (`bing.com/indexnow`).
- **Data problem:** the gap is computed by joining Google queries to Bing queries. The Bing page field is blank (`2026-06-01.md:107`, `2026-06-12.md:120`). Yet Bing reports 272 impressions on the Spanish page (`2026-06-02.md:82`), so "missing" is not the same as "not indexed".
- **Impact:** about 20 nights of "submit IndexNow" with no verified result. Meanwhile Bing is 42–59% of clicks.
- **Fix:** one script, run post-deploy, with the canonical host:
```diff
+ # scripts/indexnow.sh (run in CI after deploy)
+ curl -s -X POST https://api.indexnow.org/indexnow -H 'Content-Type: application/json; charset=utf-8' \
+  -d "{\"host\":\"www.lexiclash.live\",\"key\":\"$INDEXNOW_KEY\",\"keyLocation\":\"https://www.lexiclash.live/$INDEXNOW_KEY.txt\",\"urlList\":$(node scripts/sitemap-urls.js)}"
```
Verify with the Bing URL Inspection tool rather than the query-join.

## 5. hreflang and canonical are asserted but never shown, and slugs are not localized
`2026-06-10.md:109` ("all verified"), `2026-06-04.md:52`, `2026-05-25.md:101`

- **Evidence for risk:**
  - The Swedish landing page lives at `/sv/swedish-multiplayer-word-game`, an English slug under an `/sv/` prefix.
  - The route is `app/[locale]/…`, so `/en/juego-de-palabras-multijugador` probably also renders.
  - A hardcoded `/en/` link leaked into other locales (`2026-06-04.md:52`).
  - The sitemap was missing the best-games page and the Swedish page until 05-25.
- **Impact:** duplicate or cross-locale pages split signals on your single biggest asset.
- **Fix** in each landing `generateMetadata` (apply to all locale-specific slugs):
```diff
+ alternates: {
+   canonical: `https://www.lexiclash.live/${locale}/${slug}`,
+   languages: { es: '/es/juego-de-palabras-multijugador', sv: '/sv/spela-scrabble-online', 'x-default': '/en/multiplayer-word-game-online' },
+ },
```
Return 404 or a 301 for a slug requested under a locale it doesn't belong to. Add all localized slugs to `sitemap.ts` with `alternates.languages`.

## 6. `/en/leaderboard` has no FAQPage, though the reports disagree on whether it does
`2026-05-21.md:66` ("no FAQPage"), `2026-05-22.md:53`, `2026-05-23.md:138` (deferred), `2026-06-06.md:47`, `2026-06-18.md:71` ("deferred until n≥50")

- **Impact:** the page ranks pos 3.1–3.6 for `best competitive word games with global leaderboards` at about 0.6% CTR (`2026-06-09.md:97`). Position 3 with a thin snippet is the best AI-citation candidate on the site, and it has been deferred about 7 times.
- **Fix:** add FAQPage JSON-LD that mirrors visible text (via the `components/seo/` wrapper named in `2026-05-23.md:138`):
```diff
+ <FaqJsonLd items={[{
+  q: 'What are the best competitive word games with global leaderboards?',
+  a: 'LexiClash has daily, weekly and all-time global leaderboards across 5 languages. Daily challenges share one puzzle and reset at midnight UTC.'
+ }]} />
```
Render the same Q&A visibly on the page. Verify the "resets daily/weekly" claim first (`2026-05-21.md:70`), because the sentence will be quoted.

## 7. Internal-link equity is still thin on the pages that rank 8–10
`2026-06-18.md:46-56`, `2026-06-15.md:79`, `2026-06-04.md:80`

- **Evidence:**
  - `/he/daily` sits at pos 8.2 with 350 impressions. It has been "next lane" since 06-02.
  - `/en/daily-word-wheel` has 905 Bing impressions and no homepage link (`2026-06-02.md:95`).
  - Five blog posts pointed at a 404 `/daily/word-of-the-day` until 06-15.
- **Impact:** about 500 Hebrew impressions per 28 days at pos 8–9 with a 0.6% CTR.
- **Fix:** put crawlable `<a>` links in server-rendered HTML in the homepage hero or footer for each locale:
```diff
+ <a href={`/${locale}/daily`}>{t.daily.anchor /* he: המילה היומית */}</a>
+ <a href={`/${locale}/daily-word-wheel`}>{t.wordWheel.anchor}</a>
```
Add a link-checker step to CI to catch 404s like the one above.

## 8. Title thrash on `/en/best-online-word-games` conflicts with the data
`2026-05-22.md:79`, `2026-05-23.md:86`, `2026-05-26.md:121`, `2026-05-28.md:115`, `2026-05-28.md:126`

- **Evidence:** the title was changed 4 times in 7 days. Meanwhile the `2025/2026` queries fell 80–100%, and `2026-06-17.md:70` suggests AI Overviews took the "best" queries.
- **Impact:** the page has 1.2k impressions and 10 clicks.
- **Fix:** hold a stable title with no year prefix and rely on `dateModified` for freshness:
```diff
- title: 'Best Free Browser Word Games 2026 — 9 Honest Picks, No Download'
+ title: 'Best Free Browser Word Games — 9 Picks, No Download | LexiClash'
```
Keep the `ItemList` and `FAQPage` JSON-LD. Make the first sentence under the H1 a 40–60 word direct answer, since that is what AI engines excerpt.

## 9. Google Search Console access has been broken for about 8 nights, and the numbers are inconsistent
`2026-06-01.md:7`, `2026-06-04.md:3`, and the jump in `2026-06-18.md:4` (423 clicks vs 180 the day before)

- **Impact:** about 8 days of optimisation decisions were made on Bing-only data. The 06-18 headline looks like a different query or dimension, not growth. Every "+Δ clicks" projection in these reports (+454, +481) uses a generic CTR curve that this page has missed for a month.
- **Fix:** use a service account instead of ADC, add it as a user in GSC, and pin the dimensions:
```diff
- gcloud auth application-default login --scopes=…webmasters.readonly
+ # service account added as GSC "Restricted" user; GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS=sa.json
+ # always query: dimensions=[query,page], type=web, dataState=final
```
Stop reporting projected deltas from the expected-CTR table.

## 10. Revenue path is not visible in any SEO report
All files. Every report optimises impressions and clicks. None mention a conversion event, a signup, or what a visitor does after landing.

- **Impact:** the Spanish and Swedish pages produce the traffic, but there is no evidence anyone measures click → room created → return visit. Without that, SEO wins cannot become first revenue.
- **Fix:** add a funnel line to the daily report from PostHog: landing sessions by locale → `room_created` → `second_session_7d`. Put a single above-the-fold CTA ("Crear sala" / "Skapa rum") on the ES and SV landing pages, so the 28k impressions feed a measurable action.

## Shard 2

# SEO / GEO audit for lexiclash.live

**Evidence limit:** I was given only `docs/seo-daily/*.md`, not the source. Every `path:line` below is the report line that evidences the problem. Diffs name the files the reports mention (`he.js`, `page.tsx`, `content.ts`). They are exact in intent but unverified against the real code, so check each against the file before applying. I couldn't see `robots.txt`, the sitemap, `llms.txt` or any API/MCP surface. Items 8 and 9 are checks, not confirmed bugs.

Revenue context: Google gives about 0.6% CTR on roughly 50k impressions. Bing gives about 43% of clicks from about 8k impressions (`2026-08-02.md:14`). The biggest levers are therefore Bing indexing, the 25 days of metadata churn on one page, and the pages that don't render for crawlers.

---

## 1. `/he/daily` is client-only, with no H1 and no JSON-LD
- **Evidence:** `2026-07-29.md:106` says the page is "fully client-side rendered (DailyRedirect component). Zero static H1, zero JSON-LD."
- **Impact:** "המילה היומית" and "מילת היום" have 300–640 impressions at pos 8–9 with about 0 clicks (`2026-07-29.md:47-48`). Bing's best query was this page at 100% CTR (`2026-06-19.md:104`). Google can't extract an answer, so neither AI Overviews nor Copilot can cite it.
- **Diff (the `/he/daily` `page.tsx`, a server component):**
```diff
-export default function Page() { return <DailyRedirect /> }
+export default function Page() {
+  return (<>
+    <h1>המילה היומית — נחשו מילה בעברית ב-10 ניסיונות</h1>
+    <p>פאזל מילים יומי בעברית, חינם וללא הרשמה. אותו לוח לכולם.</p>
+    <script type="application/ld+json" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{__html: JSON.stringify(heDailyFaq)}} />
+    <DailyRedirect />
+  </>)
+}
```
  Use the three-question FAQPage draft already written at `2026-07-29.md:109-119` as `heDailyFaq`. Keep the redirect client-side so players aren't affected.

## 2. Bing has never actually received the money pages
- **Evidence:** the same gap appears for 50 days (`2026-06-19.md:46` through `2026-08-09.md:39`), with "TODO human, key needed" repeated at `2026-06-29.md:122`, `2026-07-02.md:63` and `2026-08-09.md:39`.
- **Why it matters:** `2026-08-09.md:39` says every top query is "entirely absent from Bing's own query stats". That isn't a ranking problem, it's an indexing problem. Two reports claim successful submissions (`2026-06-19.md:61`, `2026-07-04.md:58`, HTTP 202), yet the gap persists. 202 only means "accepted", and the key file may not verify.
- **Impact:** this is the cheapest source of paid-intent clicks, since Bing CTR is 6× Google's.
- **Diff:** host the key at the domain root and submit from CI after each deploy.
```diff
+# public/<INDEXNOW_KEY>.txt   (content: the key)
+# scripts/indexnow.sh, run in the deploy pipeline
+curl -sS -X POST https://api.indexnow.org/indexnow -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
+ -d '{"host":"www.lexiclash.live","key":"'$KEY'","keyLocation":"https://www.lexiclash.live/'$KEY'.txt","urlList":[ES_MP,SV_MP,HE_MP,HE_DAILY,EN_BOGGLE]}'
```
  Also check that `Sitemap:` in robots.txt is present, and verify that the ES/SV/HE URLs are in the sitemap and in Bing Webmaster's sitemap report. Use the `www.` host consistently: the reports mix `lexiclash.live` (`2026-07-16.md:61`) with `www.lexiclash.live` (`2026-07-16.md:63`), and a mismatch invalidates the key.

## 3. Stop rewriting the ES page's metadata
- **Evidence:** the title or description was rewritten on 06-18, 06-20, 06-23, 06-25, 06-26 (planned), 07-05, 07-08, 07-16, 07-19, 07-29 and 08-11, which is 11 or more rewrites in about 8 weeks. The author twice concluded "do NOT rewrite" (`2026-06-28.md:27`, `2026-08-09.md:15`) and then rewrote it again (`2026-08-11.md:123`).
- **Impact:** every rewrite resets SERP testing, and nobody can attribute a CTR change. The 12k-impression query is "scrabble online" in English, at pos 4.9 on a Spanish page (`2026-08-11.md:72`).
- **Diff:** freeze the metadata and send the English query to an English page.
```diff
 // es/juego-de-palabras-multijugador/page.tsx
+// SEO FREEZE until 2026-09-15: do not edit title/description (see docs/seo-daily)
```
  Fix the intent mismatch by linking to `/en/scrabble-alternative-online` (`2026-07-23.md:98`) from `/en` and the EN multiplayer page with the anchor "Scrabble online free". Keep the title at 60 chars or fewer, because the 77-char version was truncated (`2026-07-29.md:39`).

## 4. Locale gating and hreflang are probably broken on the multiplayer pages
- **Evidence:** `2026-06-24.md:144` fixed an `isTargetLocale` bug where non-`sv` locales were not noindexed. `2026-07-28.md:39` shows the Spanish slug under `/he/juego-de-palabras-multijugador`. Slugs are language-specific yet routed under every locale (`swedish-multiplayer-word-game` under `/sv/`). `2026-07-04.md:133` says other locales render EN copy for education pages by design.
- **Impact:** duplicate-language pages dilute the pages that rank, and the wrong locale can win the SERP slot.
- **Diff (in each `generateMetadata`):**
```diff
+alternates: { canonical: `${BASE}/${locale}/${slug}`,
+  languages: onlyRealTranslations(slug) /* omit locales that render EN fallback */ },
+robots: isTargetLocale ? undefined : { index: false, follow: true },
```
  Check that the sitemap lists only the indexable locale URLs.

```bash
grep -rn "my main motivations" app components translations
```

## 1. Title/description churn on the ES page (stop editing it)

- **Impact:** This page carries about 50k of roughly 61k impressions. Constant rewrites reset Google's snippet evaluation, and no edit can be measured. CTR has not moved in four weeks.



## 10. Agent access: `llms.txt`, robots and API/MCP are unaddressed
- **Evidence:** across 20+ reports the only agent-related items are FAQ drafts. None of them mentions `llms.txt`, AI-crawler rules, or an API, MCP server or CLI. AI-persona queries are already sending impressions (`2026-08-14.md:117`).

```diff
+# public/llms.txt
+# LexiClash
```

Longer term, expose `POST /api/rooms` returning `{roomUrl}` and wrap it in a small MCP server. That gives agents a way to start a game, not just describe one.

**Priority order for first revenue:** 1 and 2 (about 50k impressions), then 3 (cheap, with a high-CTR engine), then 4, 5 and 9, then 6–8 and 10.

**Not verified here:** the real `<head>` output, the sitemap contents, canonical tags, and whether pages are client-rendered. None of these appears in the provided files. # SEO / GEO audit: lexiclash.live

**Scope limit:** the only files I was given are `docs/seo-daily/*.md`. No `.tsx`, `next.config.mjs`, `robots`, sitemap or `llms.txt` source was included. Every `path:line` below is evidence from the reports. The code diffs target paths the reports name, and I have not seen them, so treat each as a patch template and confirm against the real file.

None of the 25 reports mention `llms.txt`, `robots.txt`, the sitemap, hreflang output, an API, an MCP server or a CLI. Those areas have never been audited.

---

## 1. One Spanish page holds about 80% of impressions at about 0.4% CTR, and English "scrabble online" lands on it

- **Impact:** about 11k impressions per 28 days from English searchers who see a Spanish title. The title has been rewritten more than 10 times without moving CTR. 0.5% in `2026-09-12.md:29` became 0.4% in `2026-10-10.md:29`.
- **Diff (new file, path assumed from the app layout):**
```diff
+ fe-next/app/[locale]/play-scrabble-online/page.tsx   // EN-only, H1 "Play Scrabble Online Free"
+   title: "Play Scrabble Online Free — Real-Time, No Signup | LexiClash"
+   alternates: { canonical: "/en/play-scrabble-online",
+     languages: { en: "/en/play-scrabble-online", es: "/es/juego-de-palabras-multijugador", "x-default": "/en/play-scrabble-online" } }
```
- Add the reverse `alternates.languages` entry on the ES page, and link the two pages to each other.

## 2. Meta edits went to files that don't reach the SERP
- **Evidence:** `2026-10-04.md:36` says the Oct 2–3 PRs edited `dailySeo.data.ts`, which only feeds JSON-LD names. `2026-10-06.md:112` says `translations/layout.ts` "has no runtime effect". `2026-10-06.md:61` says `/about` uses its own hardcoded `titleMap`/`descriptionMap`.

```diff
- app/[locale]/about/page.tsx: titleMap / descriptionMap

```

## 7. JSON-LD claims contradict each other, and the effort is aimed at deprecated rich results
- **Evidence:**
  - `2026-09-17.md:81` says the boggle page has no FAQPage, but `2026-09-12.md:75` and `2026-09-19.md:51` say it does.
  - `2026-10-08.md:75` says the WWF page's FAQ is "Unknown", but `2026-09-16.md:100` says it exists.
  - Many reports prescribe HowTo (`2026-09-16.md:104-115`, `2026-10-01…`).
- **Impact:** Google no longer shows HowTo rich results, and FAQ rich results are restricted for most sites. The value of this schema is the visible answer text that LLMs quote, not the markup. Duplicate or conflicting JSON-LD can also cause errors.
- **Diff:** validate the rendered output once, then stop adding HowTo.


## Shard 5

# SEO / GEO audit: LexiClash

**Scope limit:** I was only given the homepage route, the SEO daily reports, and unrelated Android/test/plan files. I did not see `app/sitemap.*`, `robots.*`, `public/llms.txt`, the root layout, or any API/MCP/CLI code. Items 1, 5 and 7 are therefore "not present in what I can see". Verify each with `curl` before merging.

The money pages are the ES Scrabble page (about 28k impressions/28d at about 0.4% CTR) and the EN Boggle and Words With Friends pages. Everything below is ordered by likely impact on those.

---

### 1. Homepage metadata has no canonical or hreflang
`fe-next/app/[locale]/(home)/page.tsx:69-76`

`generateMetadata` returns only title, description and keywords. If the root layout also sets no `alternates`, six locale homepages have no self-canonical and no hreflang cluster.
- Google can pick the wrong locale for `/es` and `/sv` queries.
- Query-string variants such as `?room=`, `?next=` and `utm_*` are all crawlable duplicates of the homepage.

If the layout does set a canonical, it would be inherited by every page, which is worse. Set it per page:

```diff
 export async function generateMetadata({ params }: PageProps): Promise<Metadata> {
   const { locale } = await params;
+  const base = 'https://www.lexiclash.live';
   return {
     title: { absolute: titleMap[locale] || titleMap.en },
     description: descriptionMap[locale] || descriptionMap.en,
-    keywords: keywordsMap[locale] || keywordsMap.en,
+    alternates: {
+      canonical: `${base}/${locale}`,
+      languages: {
+        ...Object.fromEntries(SUPPORTED_LOCALES.map((l) => [l, `${base}/${l}`])),
+        'x-default': `${base}/en`,
+      },
+    },
   };
 }
```

I dropped `keywords` because Google and Bing ignore it. Do the same on `/es/juego-de-palabras-multijugador` and the other landing pages. Those files weren't provided, so the diff is the same pattern.

### 2. Footer and nav links are client-only
`fe-next/app/[locale]/LocaleBodyChrome.tsx:28-31, 61`

`SiteExtras` (footer and nav) is loaded with `ssr: false`. GPTBot, ClaudeBot and PerplexityBot do not execute JS, and Googlebot's rendering is deferred. The sitewide internal links to the money pages are therefore absent from the server HTML on every page except the homepage, which carries its own links (`page.tsx:111-114`).

The reports repeatedly say "add internal links" for rank-ups (10-12, 10-15 and 10-17). The links may be there for users and invisible to crawlers.

```diff
+// components/seo/ServerFooterLinks.tsx — server component, plain <a> list:
+// /play-boggle-online-free, /online-word-games-with-friends,
+// /es/juego-de-palabras-multijugador, /sv/tools/word-solver, /blog
-        {siteChrome && <SiteExtras lang={lang} />}
+        {siteChrome && <SiteExtras lang={lang} />}
+        <ServerFooterLinks lang={lang} />
```

Keep the interactive extras lazy. Only the link list needs to be in the HTML.

### 3. Every page was served `no-store`, so ISR and CDN caching are inert
`fe-next/app/[locale]/(home)/page.tsx:16-33`

The author's own comment says production answered `cache-control: private, no-store` on SEO pages. The cause is the `fetchCache = 'force-no-store'` workaround in `layout.tsx`. That means slow TTFB for crawlers and no CDN caching on the pages that earn impressions.

Until upstream fixes it, scope the workaround away from static SEO routes.

```diff
 // app/[locale]/layout.tsx
-export const fetchCache = 'force-no-store';
+// remove; add `export const fetchCache = 'force-no-store'` only to the
+// dynamic game/admin route segments that actually leak memory
```

Confirm with `curl -sI https://www.lexiclash.live/en/play-boggle-online-free | grep -i cache-control`.

### 4. Title and description churn on the ES page is blocking measurement
`docs/seo-daily/2026-10-11.md:44`, `2026-10-12.md:44`, `2026-10-15.md:25`, `2026-10-17.md:61`

The ES Scrabble page's title was rewritten four times in seven days (Oct 11, 12, 14 and 17). Each change resets Google's snippet testing. The 28d data window overlaps all the changes, so no uplift can be attributed. The daily agent keeps re-proposing the same fix.

- **Action:** freeze title and description on `/es/juego-de-palabras-multijugador` until at least 2026-11-14.
- **Data caveat:** avg position is reported as 7.7 on 10-11 and 15.2 on 10-12 for near-identical data. Treat position claims as unreliable.
- **Process:** add a `docs/seo-daily/FREEZE.md` list that the agent must read before opening a meta PR.

### 5. Structured data: FAQPage only, and the reports misjudge its value
`fe-next/app/[locale]/(home)/page.tsx:109`; `docs/seo-daily/2026-10-12.md:102`, `2026-10-15.md:110`

Only `FaqPageJsonLd` is visible here. Root-layout schema was not provided, so Organization and WebSite may exist there.

- **Rich results:** since 2023, FAQ rich results show only for government and health sites. The reports' plan to fix CTR with more FAQPage markup will not change the SERP snippet.
- **AI citation:** FAQ copy on the page still helps LLM citation, but it is not a CTR lever.
- **What to add:** a `WebApplication` entity gives engines and agents an explicit "free, browser, no install" fact.

```diff
+<script type="application/ld+json" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: JSON.stringify({
+  '@context': 'https://schema.org',
+  '@type': 'WebApplication',
+  name: 'LexiClash',
+  url: `https://www.lexiclash.live/${locale}`,
+  applicationCategory: 'GameApplication',
+  operatingSystem: 'Any (web browser)',
+  inLanguage: locale,
+  offers: { '@type': 'Offer', price: '0', priceCurrency: 'USD' },
+}) }} />
 <FaqPageJsonLd ... />
```

### 6. Contradictory facts in the copy that AI engines quote
`fe-next/app/[locale]/(home)/seoContent.ts:33, 25, 148, 212, 252`; `docs/seo-daily/2026-10-16.md:104-106`

| Claim | Where |
|---|---|
| "Five language support:" followed by six languages | `seoContent.ts:33` |
| Adventure Mode has 100 levels | `seoContent.ts:25, 30` |
| Adventure Mode has "50+" levels | he `:148`, sv `:212`, ja `:252`, es `:292` |
| Max players is 50 | FAQ `:82` |
| Max players is "2 to 20+" | features `:27` |
| Max players is "2–20" | meta in the 10-16 report |
| Max players was "up to 4" on `/en/free-multiplayer-word-game` until 10-16 | the 10-16 report's own text |

Conflicting numbers lower trust and make an LLM less likely to cite the page. Pick one canonical number per fact.

```diff
-      'Five language support: English, Hebrew (RTL), Swedish, Japanese, Spanish, and Russian',
+      'Six language support: English, Hebrew (RTL), Swedish, Japanese, Spanish, and Russian',
```

Also align the "50+" levels strings in he, sv, ja and es to 100. The "no ads" claim at `:52` ("no paywalls, ads-to-unlock mechanics") should be checked against the AdMob integration in the Android manifest and the AdSense plan referenced at `page.tsx:118`.

### 7. No agent entry point is visible (llms.txt, API, MCP or CLI)
Nothing in the provided files exposes a machine-readable description. The only API route is `/api/version`, referenced in `NotFoundClient.tsx:35`. The product is a real-time browser game, so the most valuable agent action is "create a room and get a join link".

Minimum cheap step, a static `public/llms.txt`:

```diff
+# LexiClash
+> Free browser multiplayer word game (Boggle-style). No download or signup. 6 languages.
+## Play
+- [Boggle online free](https://www.lexiclash.live/en/play-boggle-online-free): real-time, 2–50 players
+- [Words With Friends alternative](https://www.lexiclash.live/en/online-word-games-with-friends)
+- [Scrabble online en español](https://www.lexiclash.live/es/juego-de-palabras-multijugador)
+- [Swedish anagram solver](https://www.lexiclash.live/sv/tools/word-solver)
+## Facts
+- Free, no account. Rooms shared via link: https://www.lexiclash.live/en/multiplayer?room=CODE
```

Later, a `POST /api/rooms` that returns a join URL is the natural hook for an MCP tool. Scope that after item 3.

### 8. Bing is blind, and ChatGPT and Copilot citations depend on Bing's index
`docs/seo-daily/2026-10-11.md:21, 98`

Every report since 10-11 says Bing is unavailable (proxy 403 on `ssl.bing.com`). ChatGPT search draws on Bing's index, so no Bing data means no signal on the largest AI-citation channel.

- Add `ssl.bing.com` to the proxy allowlist.
- An `IndexNowPanel` exists in admin (referenced in the admin tests). Confirm it is wired to an automated post-deploy ping, not only a manual panel.
- Verify the site in Bing Webmaster Tools.

### 9. Titles and descriptions over the display limit, and mismatched copy
`fe-next/app/[locale]/(home)/page.tsx:62-65`

The he, sv, ja and es descriptions run roughly 150–175 characters. The 10-15 and 10-16 reports already found Google truncating the CTA on other pages for the same reason.

```diff
-  sv: 'Spela gratis ordspel online med vänner — ingen nedladdning. Realtids ordstrider, dagligt ordhjul, 6 spellägen, 6 språk. Som Alfapet möter Boggle. Spela nu →',
+  sv: 'Spela gratis ordspel online med vänner — ingen nedladdning. Realtidsstrider, dagligt ordhjul, 6 språk. Som Alfapet möter Boggle. Spela nu →',
```

This is low impact on its own. Pair it with item 1's PR.

**Suggested order for a solo founder:**
1. Items 1 and 6, about 30 minutes and low risk.
2. Item 4: freeze the ES page and stop rewriting it.
3. Item 2, then item 3.

## Shard 6

# SEO and agent-legibility audit

I could only audit the files shown, which cover `app/[locale]/` from `SiteExtras` through `admin/content`. I did not see `robots.ts`, `sitemap.ts`, `public/llms.txt`, any API/MCP surface, or `LocaleBodyChrome.tsx` itself. Anything outside those files is flagged as unverified, not assumed.

## 1. Sitewide footer links are missing from the server HTML
**`fe-next/app/[locale]/SiteExtras.tsx:48`** (loaded with `ssr:false`, asserted at `LocaleBodyChrome.split.test.ts:12-14`)

- **Impact:** Sitewide navigation is client-only. `SiteFooter` is the one place that links every landing page, blog, FAQ, about and education page from every URL.
  - Googlebot renders JS, but it does so on a delay.
  - Most AI crawlers do not execute JS at all, so on every page they see zero footer links.
  - Landing pages are then reachable only through whatever links exist in each page's own body, which weakens internal link equity and discovery. This is the biggest legibility gap shown.
- **Fix:** render the link list on the server and keep only the interactive extras client-side. `SiteFooterLinks` below is a new server component to create; reuse the data source behind `SiteFooter`.
```diff
--- a/fe-next/app/[locale]/SiteExtras.tsx
-import SiteFooter from '@/components/navigation/SiteFooter';
@@
-      <SiteFooter className="relative z-0 shrink-0" />
```
```diff
--- a/fe-next/app/[locale]/layout.tsx   (server component)
+import SiteFooterLinks from '@/components/navigation/SiteFooterLinks'; // new server component: plain <nav><a href> list
@@
+        <SiteFooterLinks locale={locale} />   // hide on heavy-game paths via CSS or a pathname-free wrapper
```
The heavy-path exclusion is a client decision. Render the footer links in the server layout and hide them with a wrapper class set by `LocaleBodyChrome`.

## 2. About page: localized metadata over an English body, and `ru` falls back to English
**`about/page.tsx:12-26,30-31`; `about/PageClient.tsx:58`; `about/content.ts:42`**

- **Impact:**
  - `contentByLocale` only has `en`, so he/sv/ja/es/ru render an English body (`PageClient.tsx:58`). Each of those URLs is still self-canonical and listed in hreflang (`page.tsx:51-77`).
  - Google sees a mismatch between the localized title and the English body. These become duplicate or soft-duplicate URLs and dilute the hreflang cluster.
  - `titleMap`/`descriptionMap` have no `ru`. `page.tsx` metadata overrides the localized `about/layout.tsx` metadata, which does handle `ru`, so the Russian page gets an English title and description.
- **Fix, cheapest:** point non-English locales at the English canonical until real translations exist, and drop their hreflang entries.
```diff
--- a/fe-next/app/[locale]/about/page.tsx
+const HAS_BODY = new Set(['en']);
@@ export async function generateMetadata
-  const title = titleMap[locale] || titleMap.en;
+  const title = titleMap[locale] || titleMap.en;
@@
-      canonical: `https://www.lexiclash.live/${locale}/about`,
+      canonical: `https://www.lexiclash.live/${HAS_BODY.has(locale) ? locale : 'en'}/about`,
@@ return {
+    robots: HAS_BODY.has(locale) ? { index: true, follow: true } : { index: false, follow: true },
```
Also delete the duplicate `generateMetadata` in `about/layout.tsx:11-79`. It is dead code, since the page overrides it.

## 3. The Person schema points to URLs that do not exist
**`about/ohad-fisher/page.tsx:91,93,100-101`**

- **Impact:** `@id`, `url` and `sameAs` use `${SITE_URL}/about/ohad-fisher` with no locale segment, while the real page is `/en/about/ohad-fisher`.
  - A locale-less URL is likely a redirect or 404, so the entity does not resolve to the indexed page.
  - `sameAs` lists the page itself plus `/editorial-policy`. Neither is an external profile, so it adds no entity disambiguation.
  - For E-E-A-T and LLM author attribution, the founder and organization entity is currently unanchored.
- **Fix** (the social URL below is a placeholder; replace it with the founder's actual profile URLs):
```diff
-        '@id': `${SITE_URL}/about/ohad-fisher#person`,
+        '@id': `${SITE_URL}/en/about/ohad-fisher#person`,
@@
-        url: `${SITE_URL}/about/ohad-fisher`,
+        url: `${SITE_URL}/en/about/ohad-fisher`,
@@
-        sameAs: [
-            `${SITE_URL}/about/ohad-fisher`,
-            `${SITE_URL}/editorial-policy`,
-        ],
+        mainEntityOfPage: `${SITE_URL}/en/about/ohad-fisher`,
+        sameAs: ['https://www.instagram.com/lexi.clash' /* + LinkedIn/X for the founder */],
@@ worksFor
-            url: SITE_URL,
+            '@id': `${SITE_URL}/#organization`,
+            url: SITE_URL,
```

## 4. The author page sends conflicting indexing signals for non-English locales
**`about/ohad-fisher/page.tsx:70-81`**

- **Impact:** non-English locales are `noindex`, canonical to `/en`, and also listed in hreflang. Google ignores hreflang on noindexed pages and may treat the combination as an error.
  - OG `url` (line 65) is locale-specific while the canonical is `/en`, so the two disagree.
  - It is harmless to impressions but pollutes the Search Console coverage report.
- **Fix:**
```diff
-            languages: {
-                'x-default': ..., he: ..., en: ..., sv: ..., ja: ..., es: ..., ru: ...
-            },
+            languages: { 'x-default': `${SITE_URL}/en/about/ohad-fisher`, en: `${SITE_URL}/en/about/ohad-fisher` },
@@
-            url,
+            url: `${SITE_URL}/en/about/ohad-fisher`,
```

## 5. Play surfaces carry their copy only in JSON-LD
**Per `__tests__/appShellSeoContent.test.tsx:19-85` (page files not shown)**

- **Impact:** `/multiplayer`, `/brain` and `/daily` deliberately render no visible text, and the test pins `textContent === ''` for `he`.
  - Crawlers and LLMs get chrome only, plus JSON-LD whose FAQ text does not match any visible content. Google may ignore it, and it does not count as citable page content.
  - The UX decision is fine. The consequence is that ranking must come from the dedicated landings, which makes the footer links (issue 1) and landing-to-landing links more important.
- **Fix:** add a one-line server-rendered crawlable link from each play surface to its landing, then pin it in the test.
```diff
--- a/fe-next/app/[locale]/multiplayer/page.tsx
+      <a href={`/${locale}/free-multiplayer-word-game`} className="sr-only">Free multiplayer word game: rules and modes</a>
```
```diff
--- a/__tests__/appShellSeoContent.test.tsx
-    expect(el.textContent?.trim()).toBe('');
+    expect(el.querySelector('a[href*="free-multiplayer-word-game"]')).not.toBeNull();
```
An `sr-only` link is a legitimate link, not hidden keyword text. Keep it to the link and never duplicate the FAQ.

## 6. Organization schema is thin and omits Russian
**`about/page.tsx:108,95-116`**

- **Impact:**
  - `knowsLanguage` omits `ru`, even though `availableLanguage` includes Russian.
  - There is no `sameAs`, so LLM knowledge graphs cannot link the brand to Instagram or the app stores.
  - Organization is emitted only on `/about`, not sitewide, so the `@id` reference from other pages' JSON-LD may dangle.
- **Fix:**
```diff
-      knowsLanguage: ['en', 'he', 'sv', 'ja', 'es'],
+      knowsLanguage: ['en', 'he', 'sv', 'ja', 'es', 'ru'],
+      sameAs: ['https://www.instagram.com/lexi.clash', 'https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=live.lexiclash.app'],
+      founder: { '@id': 'https://www.lexiclash.live/en/about/ohad-fisher#person' },
```
Also emit this block in the root locale layout so every page resolves it.

## 7. Admin routes lack `noindex`
**`admin/analytics/page.tsx:1-6`, `admin/blocklist/page.tsx:1-5`, `admin/content/page.tsx:1-6`**

- **Impact:** only `connections-review` sets `robots` (`connections-review/page.tsx:8`). The others rely on the layout redirect for non-admins.
  - Googlebot gets a 307 to `/en`, which wastes crawl budget and shows up as redirect noise in Search Console.
  - Put the guard in the shared admin layout so new admin pages are covered by default.
- **Fix** (in `admin/layout.tsx`; the file was not shown, only its test):
```diff
+export const metadata = { robots: { index: false, follow: false } };
```
Also confirm `Disallow: /*/admin` in `robots.ts` (not visible).

## 8. `/accessibility` is `force-dynamic` for a static page
**`accessibility/page.tsx:5`**

- **Impact:** the page is fully client-side with static metadata. `force-dynamic` ships `no-store` and a per-request render, and the repo's own test (`prerenderedLocaleRoutes.test.ts:7-24`) documents this pattern as a TTFB and crawl-efficiency cost. The effect is small.
- **Fix:**
```diff
-export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';
+export const revalidate = 86400;
+export function generateStaticParams() { return SUPPORTED_LOCALES.map((locale) => ({ locale })); }
```
Import `SUPPORTED_LOCALES` from `@/lib/localeResolution`.

## 9. Not verifiable from these files: llms.txt, sitemap/robots, API, MCP, CLI
- Nothing shown defines `llms.txt`, `robots.ts`, `sitemap.ts`, or an agent entry point (API, MCP or CLI). `/api/account/delete` is the only API route visible, and it is auth-only.
- Send me `app/robots.ts`, `app/sitemap.ts`, `public/llms.txt` and `next.config.mjs` (the slug redirects) for the next pass.

# SEO / GEO audit: `ohadf2015/boggle-new`

**Scope caveat:** the diff shows only `admin/*`, `adventure/*`, `ai-fanfare-demo` and `anagram/[letters]`. I cannot see `app/sitemap.ts`, `robots.ts`, `public/llms.txt`, `lib/seo/generatePageMetadata.ts`, the home page, or any MCP/API manifest. Findings 5 and 6 are therefore "verify" items, not confirmed defects. The admin, demo and anagram noindex handling is correct, so I did not list it.

## 1. Conflicting game facts: 70 levels vs 100 levels
- **Where:** `fe-next/app/[locale]/adventure/layout.tsx:12-17` against `fe-next/app/[locale]/adventure/page.tsx:21-25`.
- **Problem:** the layout metadata and OG text say "70 levels in 10 themed worlds". The on-page H1, FAQ and JSON-LD say "100 levels".
- **Impact:** this is the highest-priority fix. Search snippets, `VideoGameJsonLd` and the visible copy disagree, which weakens AI citations. An LLM that extracts the fact will see contradictory numbers and may drop the entity or quote the wrong one. It can also read as a quality problem during AdSense review.
- **Diff:** pick the true number (the code and FAQ say 100) and use it everywhere.
```diff
- 'Embark on an epic word adventure! Battle word bosses across 70 levels in 10 themed worlds and win a new tile skin from every boss. No download needed.',
+ 'Embark on an epic word adventure! Battle word bosses across 100 levels in 10 themed worlds and win a new tile skin from every boss. No download needed.',
```

## 2. Duplicate, drifting metadata in the layout and the page
- **Where:** `adventure/layout.tsx:20-91` (hand-rolled `generateMetadata`) and `adventure/page.tsx:13-17` (`generatePageMetadata`).
- **Problems:**
  - Two sources of truth exist, and the page-level one normally wins. The layout copy is dead or conflicting, which is how the 70 vs 100 drift in finding 1 happened.
  - `ru` is in `alternates.languages` (line 66), but the `Locale` type and the allow-list (lines 5, 22) exclude it. A `/ru/adventure` request gets English title and description.
  - 17 region variants (`en-IL`, `en-JP`, `en-AU`, `es-AR`…) all point at the same few URLs. These are noisy hreflang entries with no benefit. Only the `x-default` entry plus one per language is needed.
- **Impact:** the wrong-language snippet on `/ru` reduces CTR. A bloated hreflang block adds crawl noise and a risk of Search Console "no return tag" warnings.
- **Diff:** delete the layout's metadata and keep only the pass-through.
```diff
-import { loadTranslation } from '@/translations/loadTranslation';
-import type { Metadata } from 'next';
 import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
-type Locale = ...
-const EN_ADVENTURE_SEO = {...};
-export async function generateMetadata(...) {...}
```
Then make sure `generatePageMetadata` emits `languages` for `en, he, sv, ja, es, ru` plus `x-default`, with no region duplicates.

## 3. `/adventure` forces dynamic rendering on an indexed landing page
- **Where:** `adventure/page.tsx:11` (`export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'`). The same flag is on `adventure/achievements/page.tsx:11`.
- **Problem:** the page body is static (a locale-keyed content map plus a translation load), yet every crawl and every AI fetch hits the server with no caching.
- **Impact:** slower TTFB hurts crawl rate and Core Web Vitals. AI fetchers with short timeouts may skip the page.
- **Diff:**
```diff
-export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';
+export const revalidate = 86400;
+export function generateStaticParams() {
+  return ['en','he','sv','ja','es','ru'].map((locale) => ({ locale }));
+}
```
Do this only if nothing in the page reads cookies or headers. `PageClient` is client-side and handles auth, so it should be fine.

## 4. Achievements page renders nothing on the server
- **Where:** `adventure/achievements/AchievementsPageClient.tsx:33-48`.
- **Problem:** `if (isResolving) return null;` means SSR HTML is empty, because `loading` starts true. A crawler or LLM fetcher gets a blank body, while the page is advertised through `generateMetadata` with its own SEO key.
- **Impact:** this is a thin or empty indexed URL, which Google may drop as a soft 404. It also contributes nothing to citations.
- **Diff:** don't block guests, and show a skeleton instead of null.
```diff
-  const isResolving = loading || (!!user && !profile);
...
-  if (isResolving) return null;
+  // Guests render the catalog immediately; only per-user progress waits on auth.
```
Alternatively, mark this route `noIndex: true` in `generatePageMetadata` (line 15) if it is not meant to rank.

## 5. The playable game is `ssr:false`
- **Where:** `adventure/PageClient.tsx:24-30`.
- **Problem:** `AdventureView` is client-only. The SEO copy in `GamePageSeoContent` carries indexing, which is fine. But agents and crawlers see only a loader plus prose, with no link to a machine-readable description of the product.
- **Impact:** low for Google, medium for agents. Nothing exposes "how to play or integrate" in a structured way.
- **Diff:** keep `ssr:false` for the game, and make sure the server-rendered `GamePageSeoContent` contains internal links to `/{locale}/singleplayer`, `/tools/word-solver` and `/words/*`. Page 3's anagram template does this and the adventure page does not.

## 6. Agent reachability (could not verify, probably missing)
- **Evidence:** `anagram/[letters]/page.tsx:8` imports `@/app/api/word-solver/dictionaryLoader`, so a word-solver API exists. The shown files contain no `llms.txt`, MCP server, OpenAPI document or CLI.
- **Impact:** AI assistants asked "solve this Boggle board" or "find anagrams of X" have nothing structured to call or cite. This is the only GEO upside left now that the programmatic anagram pages are noindexed.
- **Diff:** if `public/llms.txt` is absent, add it (verify the URLs exist first):
```diff
+# LexiClash
+> Free multiplayer Boggle-style word game in 6 languages (en, he, sv, ja, es, ru). Daily puzzle, solo, multiplayer, and a 100-level RPG Adventure.
+
+## Play
+- [Adventure](https://www.lexiclash.live/en/adventure): 100-level RPG word puzzle, free
+- [Solo](https://www.lexiclash.live/en/singleplayer)
+- [Multiplayer](https://www.lexiclash.live/en/multiplayer)
+
+## Tools
+- [Word Solver](https://www.lexiclash.live/en/tools/word-solver): finds all words on a Boggle board
+
+## Optional
+- [Sitemap](https://www.lexiclash.live/sitemap.xml)
```
Also document the word-solver endpoint (an OpenAPI stub) and reference it from `llms.txt`.

## 7. Anagram family: JSON-LD and dictionary work on noindexed pages
- **Where:** `anagram/[letters]/page.tsx:11` (`force-dynamic`), `:122-154` (4 JSON-LD blocks), `:132-133` (full dictionary scan per request).
- **Problem:** the family is deliberately noindexed (`:47`), yet each crawl runs a dictionary scan and ships schema nobody uses.
- **Impact:** wasted server cost and crawl budget. There is no SEO upside while noindexed. Revenue-wise this is minor; do it last.
- **Diff:** either drop the JSON-LD while noindexed, or add `Disallow: /*/anagram/` to `robots.txt` if the pages stay noindexed long-term. Do not do both, since a robots disallow stops Google from seeing the noindex tag and previously indexed URLs can linger.
```diff
-      <script type="application/ld+json" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: schemaJson }} />
```

Fix findings 1 and 2 (an hour of work, and they remove contradictory facts and a wrong-language snippet). Then fix 3 and 4. Then check 6 against the unseen `public/` and `app/` roots.

## 1. `force-dynamic` defeats the ISR the page also asks for
**`fe-next/app/[locale]/anagram/page.tsx:225` and `:229`**

- **Problem:** the page sets `dynamic = 'force-dynamic'` and `revalidate = 86400`. The first wins, so every crawler and AI-bot hit renders on demand.
- **Impact:** slower TTFB and wasted crawl budget on the page you are betting on for "anagram svenska".
- **Why it's safe:** the page reads no cookies or searchParams.

```diff

-
 const BASE_URL = 'https://www.lexiclash.live';
```

## 2. The FAQ makes false or inconsistent claims, which hurts AI citations
**`anagram/page.tsx:35`, `:41`, `:51`, `:46`; `lib/anagramLogic.ts:3`, `:63`, `:88`**

- **"Runs entirely in your browser":** `anagramLogic.ts:3` says it runs server-side. Lines 35 and 41 say "runs locally in your browser… zero data sent to third parties".
- **"Returns every valid word":** line 51 says this, but `anagramLogic.ts:88` caps results at 300.
- **Scrabble claims:** line 46 says "every word… legal in both Scrabble and WWF", and line 32 promises point values and etymology. I can't verify these from this code.
- **Impact:** LLMs quote FAQ answers verbatim. Claims that contradict the page or the code get your domain discounted, or produce wrong citations.

```diff
-'Always free to play, no signup, runs entirely in your browser with zero data sent to third parties',
+'Always free to play, no signup, results rendered instantly on the server',
...
-The whole process runs locally in your browser in under 100ms.',
+The lookup runs on the server and typically returns in well under a second.',
...
-returns every valid word in one pass,
+returns up to 300 valid words in one pass, longest first,
```

Apply the same fix to the `he`, `es` and `ru` copies at lines 75, 85, 138, 157, 163, 182, 188 and 194. They are noindex but still feed `GamePageSeoContent`.

## 3. The "anagram svenska" page promises Swedish but solves English
**`anagram/page.tsx:15-16`, `:102`, `:114`, `:326`**

- **Mismatch:** the H1, the answer (line 16, "svenska ord") and the title promise Swedish anagrams. Line 102 and line 114 say the solver returns "legal engelskt ord". `parseLetters` (`anagramLogic.ts:40`) strips everything outside `a-z`, so å, ä and ö are deleted.
- **Impact:** a Swedish searcher enters "lyssna" and gets nothing useful. Pogo-sticking will erode the ranking you just unlocked by flipping `noindex`.
- **Cheapest honest fix:** stop claiming Swedish solving until it is supported. Rewrite the claim so it matches the product.

```diff
-  'Anagram svenska betyder att blanda om bokstäver till nya ord. LexiClash gratis anagramlösare på svenska tar 2–10 bokstäver och visar varje giltigt tävlingsord, ...
+  'Anagram svenska betyder att blanda om bokstäver till nya ord. LexiClash gratis anagramlösare tar 2–10 bokstäver (a–z) och visar giltiga engelska tävlingsord, grupperade efter längd. Svenska tecken (å, ä, ö) stöds ännu inte. Ingen registrering och ingen app. ...
```

The real fix is to add a Swedish dictionary and `[åäö]` to the regex. Until then, don't claim it.

## 10. Agent reachability: the solver is only a URL pattern, with no API or machine-readable contract
**`anagram/page.tsx:384`; `lib/anagramLogic.ts:38`**

- **Current state:** in the files I can see, the only agent-facing contract is the human sentence "Visit /anagram/yourletters".
- **Impact:** an LLM agent or assistant can't discover or call the solver, so it can't recommend it with confidence. Citation-ready tools win AI referrals.
- **Fix:** expose a tiny JSON route reusing `parseLetters` and `findAnagramsFromLetters`, and reference it from `llms.txt` and an OpenAPI stub. The dictionary load in this sketch is a placeholder, since I don't know where the page loads it.

```ts
// fe-next/app/api/anagram/route.ts (new)
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { parseLetters, findAnagramsFromLetters } from '@/app/[locale]/anagram/lib/anagramLogic';
import { loadDictionary } from '@/lib/dictionary'; // adjust to the loader the /anagram/[letters] page uses

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  const letters = parseLetters(req.nextUrl.searchParams.get('letters') ?? '');
  if (!letters) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'letters must be 2-10 a-z chars, max 4 of one letter' }, { status: 400 });
  const words = findAnagramsFromLetters(letters, await loadDictionary('en'));
  return NextResponse.json({ letters, count: words.length, capped: words.length === 300, words },
    { headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'public, s-maxage=86400', 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*' } });
}
```

Add to `public/llms.txt` (check whether it exists):

```
- [Anagram solver API](https://www.lexiclash.live/api/anagram?letters=listen): JSON list of valid English words from 2-10 letters.
- [Anagram solver](https://www.lexiclash.live/en/anagram): human UI, URL pattern /en/anagram/{letters}.
```

# SEO/GEO audit: lexiclash.live

**Scope limit:** I only saw 9 files. `sitemap.ts`, `robots.ts`, `llms.txt`, `BlogJsonLd`, `FaqPageJsonLd`, `VideoGameJsonLd` and the `ai-vs-word-games` `page.tsx` were not provided. I flag those gaps rather than guess at them. Findings are ordered by impact on impressions and AI citations.

## 1. Contradictory facts about the product on the page AI engines will quote
**`fe-next/app/[locale]/best-online-word-games/page.tsx:174`, `:359`, `:372` (vs `:80`, `:121`, `:172`, `:60`)**

- **Language count:** the page says "5 (EN · HE · SV · JA · ES)" at line 174 and "ESL-friendly in 5 langs" at line 359. The FAQ (lines 80, 84), the VideoGame JSON-LD (line 121) and the description (line 113) say 6 languages including Russian.
- **Ads:** the card says "Optional rewarded only" at line 172. FAQ line 60 says "a small banner and optional rewarded videos". The Quick Picks card at line 368 says "zero forced interstitial ads", which is an unqualified claim.
- **Wordle price:** line 187 says "Yes (part of NYT bundle)", the table says "Freemium", and FAQ line 60 says it lives "behind the NYT paywall".

LLMs that find two values for one entity either drop the claim or cite a competitor. The "only one" superlatives (lines 56, 64, 80) are also easy to contradict.

```diff
-    languages: '5 (EN · HE · SV · JA · ES)',
+    languages: '6 (EN · HE · SV · JA · ES · RU)',
-    ads: 'Optional rewarded only',
+    ads: 'Small banner + optional rewarded videos, no interstitials',
-sub: 'Vocabulary duels, custom word lists, teacher dashboard. Free, no student accounts. ESL-friendly in 5 langs.'
+sub: 'Vocabulary duels, custom word lists, teacher dashboard. Free, no student accounts. ESL-friendly in 6 langs.'
-{ tag: 'NO ADS, EVER', title: 'Wordle + LexiClash', sub: 'The only two on this list with zero forced interstitial ads. ...'
+{ tag: 'NO INTERSTITIALS', title: 'Wordle + LexiClash', sub: 'The only two on this list with no full-screen interstitial ads. ...'
```

Also change line 359 to `6 langs`, and soften "the only" to "one of the few" at lines 56 and 64.

For `/he/...`, `/ru/...` and the rest, the metadata sends `noindex` and a canonical to a different URL. Google treats that as a conflicting signal. The `alternates.languages` map also lists only `en`, so the hreflang cluster is incomplete. There is no cost for `en`, but the other locales are the pages that can leak into the index.

# SEO and agent-legibility audit: LexiClash blog slice

**Scope.** I only had the blog page files, `content.ts`, `faq.ts` and the `PageClient` files. I could not see `BlogJsonLd.tsx` (`generateBlogMetadata`), the sitemap, `robots.ts`, `llms.txt` or any API/MCP surface. Items 1–8 are verified from the code shown. Item 9 is a gap I could not check. Item 10 is a minor language fix.

## 1. Spanish metadata sits on an English body, with hreflang claiming a translation
`fe-next/app/[locale]/blog/best-boggle-alternatives-2026/page.tsx:23,31,39`

`metaTitles` and `metaDescriptions` have an `es` entry. `contentByLocale` (`content.ts`) and `faqByLocale` (`faq.ts`) only have `en/he/sv/ja`. Because `hasTranslation: locale in metaTitles` is true for `es`, `/es/blog/best-boggle-alternatives-2026` is indexable and advertised as Spanish. The body and the FAQPage JSON-LD are English.

- **Impact:** Spanish-query impressions go to a page that bounces users, and the page looks like thin or duplicate content. This is your highest-traffic comparison page.
- **Fix:** use the content map as the source of truth for `hasTranslation`.

```diff
-  return generateBlogMetadata({ slug: SLUG, locale, title, description, datePublished: DATE_PUBLISHED, dateModified: DATE_MODIFIED, hasTranslation: locale in metaTitles });
+  return generateBlogMetadata({ slug: SLUG, locale, title, description, datePublished: DATE_PUBLISHED, dateModified: DATE_MODIFIED, hasTranslation: locale in contentByLocale });
```

Then either delete the `es` keys at lines 23 and 31, or add `es` to `content.ts` and `faq.ts`.

`fe-next/app/[locale]/blog/boggle-vs-scrabble/content.ts:121-126`

`he`, `sv`, `ja` and `es` end with a Q&A section ("שאלות נפוצות", "Vanliga frågor" and so on). `en` does not. `extractFaqFromSections` (`page.tsx:51`) therefore returns nothing for the locale that carries the most search volume. Queries like "is boggle harder than scrabble" are exactly what AI answers quote.

- **Impact:** lost FAQ markup and lost extractable Q&A for the head query.

```diff
       },

+        title: 'Frequently Asked Questions',

+
+What is the best free online Boggle game?

+
+Is Scrabble good for learning a language?
+Yes. Scrabble trains letter patterns and word construction; Boggle trains recognition speed. Use both.`,

```

## 4. ai-vs-word-games: the title and description are too long, and the JSON-LD citations look unreliable
`fe-next/app/[locale]/blog/ai-vs-word-games-language-learning/page.tsx:19,27,61-87`

- The `en` title is about 83 characters and the description about 188. Both will be truncated in the SERP, and the "(Science + Reddit)" tail adds nothing.
- The `citations` array includes a Cambridge URL with a generic slug (`…/article/spaced-repetition-learning`) that looks invented. It also includes a Reddit community front page labelled as a "2.1M member community discussion thread".
- **Impact:** LLMs and crawlers that follow `citation` links hit a 404, and E-E-A-T drops. A claim like "30 studies" and "2.1M Reddit users" that cannot be traced hurts the one page positioned for AI citation. The `2026-06-27` publish date is also worth checking against today's date.
- **Fix:** shorten the strings and keep only citations you have verified.

```diff
+  en: 'AI Language Apps vs Word Games: Which Teaches Vocabulary Faster?',
-  en: 'Science-backed comparison of AI language apps (Duolingo, Babbel) vs word games for vocabulary learning. What 2.1M Reddit users and 30 studies actually found. Includes a practical protocol.',
+  en: 'AI language apps (Duolingo, Babbel) vs word games for vocabulary: what the research and learner discussions show, plus a practical weekly protocol.',
```

```diff
-          {
-            title: 'Spaced repetition and memory consolidation in foreign language vocabulary learning',
-            url: 'https://www.cambridge.org/core/journals/language-teaching/article/spaced-repetition-learning',
-            publisher: 'Cambridge University Press',
-          },
```

Replace the removed entry with a real DOI link, and drop the Reddit front-page "citation" at lines 82-86 or point it at a specific thread.

## 5. The H1 and `headline` don't carry the target keyword
`best-boggle-alternatives-2026/content.ts:24` and `page.tsx:19,75`

The title tag is "6 Best Boggle Alternatives & Apps in 2026". The H1 and BlogPosting `headline` is "I Tried Every Boggle Alternative I Could Find. Most of Them Suck." The `he/sv/ja` titles also disagree with their own H1s.

- **Impact:** Google often rewrites mismatched titles using the H1. The profanity also makes the page unlikely to be quoted by assistants.
- **Fix:** keep the voice but put the keyword first.

```diff
-    title: 'I Tried Every Boggle Alternative I Could Find. Most of Them Suck.',
+    title: 'Best Boggle Alternatives in 2026: I Tried Them All (Most Are Pay-to-Win)',
```

The problems:

## 7. The visible date doesn't match the structured data
- `best-boggle-alternatives-2026/PageClient.tsx:146` shows `2025-12-01`, but `DATE_MODIFIED` is `2026-05-19` (`page.tsx:16`).
- `boggle-vs-scrabble/PageClient.tsx:97` shows `2026-03-28`, but `DATE_MODIFIED` is `2026-05-19` (`page.tsx:20`).
- Dates are hardcoded in two places per page. `alternativas-a-scrabble/PageClient.tsx:97` also formats with `language` rather than `locale`.
- **Impact:** a freshness mismatch lowers trust, and LLM retrievers prefer pages with a visible "updated" date.
- **Fix:** export the dates from `page.tsx` or a shared constants file and show "Updated …".

```diff
-              {safeToLocaleDateString(new Date('2025-12-01'), locale, { month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' })}
+              {safeToLocaleDateString(new Date(DATE_MODIFIED), locale, { month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' })}
```

## 8. The author entity is inconsistent across locales
- `best-boggle-alternatives-2026/content.ts:28,186,343,498`: "Ohad Fisher" in `en`, but "חנון המילים", "Ordnörden" and "ワードオタク" in the other locales.

```diff
-    authorName: 'חנון המילים',
+    authorName: 'Ohad Fisher',
```

Pass the same name into `BlogPostingJsonLd` and add `sameAs` links (LinkedIn, GitHub) in `BlogJsonLd.tsx`.

### 2. All five locales ship to the browser, because `content.ts` is imported from a client component
`fe-next/app/[locale]/blog/*/PageClient.tsx:15` (every file shown), `content.ts` files

`fe-next/app/[locale]/blog/boggle-vs-words-with-friends/PageClient.tsx:150`

- `boggle-vs-words-with-friends/content.ts:471-583`: the entire Spanish article is unaccented ("Que es Boggle realmente?", "Cual", "dia", "busqueda"). It also uses `?` with no opening `¿`.
- Native queries use accents ("¿cuál es mejor?"). Unaccented SERP titles look machine-written and lower CTR. The unaccented body also weakens the "culturally adapted, not translated" claim in the header comment.

### 5. The Hebrew keyword is spelled two ways across sibling posts
- Wordle post: "באגל" (`boggle-vs-wordle/page.tsx:20`, `content.ts:110`).
- WWF and death-of-turn-based posts: "בוגל" (`boggle-vs-words-with-friends/page.tsx:20`, `content.ts:132`).
- This splits the ranking signal for the head term across two spellings, and the pages don't reinforce each other.
- Pick the spelling with higher search volume (check Search Console or Trends) and use it everywhere. Then make each post mention the other spelling once in the body.

### 6. Possible future-dated or suspicious dates in structured data
`death-of-turn-based-word-games/page.tsx:15-16` and `PageClient.tsx:97`
- `datePublished` and `dateModified` are both `2026-09-12`, later than every other post's `dateModified` (`2026-05-19`).

- The two sibling posts both have `DATE_MODIFIED = '2026-05-19'` (`boggle-vs-wordle/page.tsx:16`, `boggle-vs-words-with-friends/page.tsx:16`). That looks like a bulk bump without content changes, which Google discounts.
- The visible date is hard-coded separately in each `PageClient` (`daily-challenge-strategies/PageClient.tsx:110` shows `2025-07-22`), so it can drift from the JSON-LD.
- Fix by exporting the date constants from one module (`./meta.ts`) and importing them into both `page.tsx` and `PageClient`. Only bump `dateModified` on real edits.

Also add a `ru` entry to `relatedHeadingByLocale` (`PageClient.tsx:27-33`), since `metaTitles` has `ru` at line 23.

### 8. Related-article links are English-only with empty excerpts, and are duplicated per post
`boggle-vs-words-with-friends/PageClient.tsx:34-43`, `death-of-turn-based-word-games/PageClient.tsx:34-44`, `free-word-games-online/PageClient.tsx:34-44`
- Titles are hard-coded English with `excerpt: ''` and `image: ''`. On he/ja/es/sv pages the internal-link anchor text is English, which is a weak relevance signal for those locales.
- Each post maintains its own hand-written list. Slugs like `netflix-word-game-2026-rise` and `multiplayer-strategy-guide` will 404 silently if renamed.

- It uses `/images/blog/multiplayer-social.jpg` (also the hero for another post) and the `TODO-image` comment is still there.

### 10. Author attribution is inconsistent, which weakens E-E-A-T and the AI-citation entity
`boggle-vs-wordle/content.ts:26, 114, 235, 356, 477`
